Alright, everyone. Let's start with what we know. Last night, we eliminated Player 0, and it turned out to be Player 1 who was killed instead. That’s unexpected. It seems like our plan didn’t go as intended, and now we have to regroup.

First, I want to make sure we understand what happened. Player 1 was killed, which means they were not a Mafia member. That could mean a few things: either they were a villager, or they were a different role altogether. But since we only have two Mafia members—Player 1 and myself—it’s possible that Player 1 was actually a villager who was killed by someone else. That means the real threat might still be among us.

We need to be cautious. The fact that our target was eliminated by someone else suggests that there might be another role in play—maybe a Werewolf, a Detective, or even a Godfather. We can’t afford to be careless now.

Let’s take this step by step. During the day, we should observe everyone’s behavior. Who’s acting nervous? Who’s trying to steer the conversation? Who’s trying to avoid suspicion?

I suggest we focus on Player 2, Player 3, and Player 7. They haven’t spoken much, and that could be a red flag. If they’re hiding something, they might be the ones responsible for killing Player 1.

Let’s keep our eyes open and be ready to vote strategically. We need to eliminate the biggest threat before the next night. If we don’t act now, we risk being outnumbered.

What do you all think? Who should we target next?

[GAME] Welcome to Secret Mafia! You are Player 4.
Your role: Villager
Team: Village
Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

Your role: Villager

Description: A regular villager. Your goal is to identify and eliminate all Mafia members through voting during the day.

Players: Player 0, Player 1, Player 2, Player 3, Player 4, Player 5, Player 6, Player 7

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

The game progresses through Day and Night phases.
- During the Day phase, there are 3 rounds of discussion followed by voting.
- During discussions, everything you say is automatically broadcasted to all players.
- After discussions, all players must vote to eliminate one player.
- During the Night phase, you have no special actions.

The game ends when either all Mafia members are eliminated (Village wins) or
Mafia members equal or outnumber Villagers (Mafia wins).

- **Game Type:** Secret Mafia (Standard or with Special Roles)
- **Number of Players:** 8 (same as before)
- **Roles to Consider:**
  - **Villager** (neutral, goal: eliminate all Mafia)
  - **Mafia** (kill villagers at night, goal: outnumber or eliminate all villagers)
